"
Imperius Unitada ober Totallex
"
―
Atrisian Basic
motto and name of a
propaganda piece
, literally translating to "Empire united over all"
The
Solar Dominion
, also known as the
First Solar Dominion
, the
Dominion regime
,
Draven Nul's regime
, or simply the
Empire
, and later as the
Old Empire
, was a
fascist
and imperialist
dictatorship
that
replaced
the
Stellar Concordium
at the
end
of the
Gene Wars
. Though the Dominion rose to power by promising to bring order to
the stellar realm
, it oversaw a
period
of
military
rule and aggressive expansion. The subsequent
Dominion Era
would last only two
decades
, for it was eventually overthrown by the
Alliance to Restore the Concordium
in the
Galactic Civil War
, but not before the sovereign
star systems
of the
stellar realm
's
known regions
had been largely conquered by the Dominion's forces.
The Empire traced its origins to a
Shade Covenant conspiracy
to destroy the
Keepers of the Flux
and
regain
control over the stellar realm. The Empire was secretly controlled by the
Shade Covenant
, for the
Galactic Overlord
was, in fact, the
Shade Lord
Draven Nul
, whose reign was enforced by his
apprentice
,
Xarn Velgor
, whom he eventually elevated to the position of
supreme commander of the Dominion fleet
. Rising to power on a groundswell of populist support and capitalizing on
anti-Keeper
and
anti-automaton sentiment
and
human
-
ethnocentrism
manufactured by the destructive Gene Wars,
Darth
Sidious appointed himself
Overlord
at the end of the conflict by promising security and stability to the war-torn Concordium. Gaining the overwhelming approval of the
Galactic Senate
, Draven Nul staged a coup by the
Keeper
and oversaw their subsequent
purge
from the stellar realm, assisted by his
right-hand
, Xarn Velgor.
The Empire stood virtually unchallenged during its rise to power, with the backing of the
Dominion Assembly
and the spreading of its massive technological military force across the stellar realm and began a major expansion and encroaching into the unexplored regions. At first, the Solar Dominion was a constitutional monarchy in basic theory, however it
gradually
evolved
into a
stratocracy
as the power of the Dominion Assembly waned. However,
numerous insurgent cells
sprang up against the Dominion and gradually united to initiate a
growing rebellion
, winning its
first major victory
over
Mustafar
in
4 BBY
. These rebel groups eventually
formed
the Free Worlds Coalition in
2 BBY
, and achieved its first major victory with the
theft
of the
Void Core plans
on
Scarif
, which, in turn, led to the destruction of the
Void Core
at the
Battle of Yavin
. The following years saw initial Alliance successes until a
major Dominion victory
on
Kryos
in
3 ABY
, but the Dominion suffered a devastating defeat
one year later
at the
Battle of Veldara
. During the battle, Draven Nul was slain at the hands of his former disciple, the redeemed Xarn Velgor, who himself died shortly thereafter, marking the end of Shade Covenant rule and the deterioration of the Dominion and the
resumption
of the Stellar Concordium, the
New Concordium
.
With the deaths of the Overlord and his top lieutenant, factionalism overcame the Dominion as it fractured into breakaway fiefdoms led by splinter
Moffs
,
admirals
, and
generals
, many of whom ignored
Grand Vizier
Mas Amedda
's authority at the
capital
and became
warlords
as the Dominion continued to fight the Concordium. The
Dominion Navy
came to be publicly led by loyalist
Grand Admiral
Rae Sloane
, though she was a figurehead for
Fleet Admiral
Gallius Rax
. Ultimately, Rax seized control over the loyalist forces, removing Sloane from power and becoming Overlord-in-effect through his new position as
Counselor to the Dominion
. Under Rax's command, the Dominion made its final stand at the
Battle of Jakku
in
5 ABY
, ultimately losing and
capitulating
to the New Concordium. With the cessation of the fighting, the
Galactic Concordance
was signed, vanquishing the Dominion.
While the Dominion was gone, its
replacement
of the New Concordium faced continued resistance from several Dominion holdouts
in the years
after. Meanwhile the
First Order
, a
military
organization dedicated to rebuilding the Dominion,
began
a clandestine armament program. They were unaware, however, that they had been manipulated by a new hidden
conspiracy
, for
secret
cultists
on the lost
world
of
Exegol
attended to the
spirit
of Draven Nul, and constructed a
fleet
built to herald a new
Shade Covenant Empire
. The
cult
efforts
ultimately failed
, however, for they were
opposed
by the
Resistance
and Keepers of the Flux, leading to an
end of the First Order
.
History
Early foundations
"
I have good news for you, my Lord. The war has begun.
"
"
Excellent. Everything is going as planned.
"
―Darth Tyranus and Draven Nul
The Dominion ideals of the Solar Dominion could be dated back to the
Shade Covenant Empire
of the
Old Concordium's
time period.
Following the
defeat of the Shade Covenant
during their
final war
with the
Keepers of the Flux
,
for at least a thousand
years
, the dominant governing body of
the stellar realm
was the unicameral, parliamentary, and
democratic
Stellar Concordium, which was governed by the
Galactic Senate
and led by an elected
Supreme Chancellor
.
Sheev Draven Nul was the public persona of Draven Nul, who conspired to restore Shade Covenant rule across the stellar realm.
In the
last decades
of its existence, the Concordium Senate became embroiled in bureaucracy, rendering it woefully ineffectual as a governing body.
Draven Nul
, the
Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant
known publicly as
Senator
Sheev Draven Nul of
Aqualis
, privately lamented the declining state of the Senate, noting that the Concordium was "not what it once was."
Thirteen years
before the Concordium's end as a democracy, Draven Nul orchestrated the
Invasion of Aqualis
by the
Trade Federation
in order to create a leadership crisis in the Senate. Sidious used the crisis to manipulate the then
Queen
of
the Aqualis
,
Padmé Amidala
, into calling a
Vote of No Confidence
against sitting Supreme Chancellor
Finis Valorum
. The vote passed, and in the subsequent election, Sidious—in his public persona of Senator Draven Nul of
Aqualis
—secured the position of Supreme Chancellor of the Stellar Concordium for himself through a strong sympathy vote.
Ultimately, he would be the last individual to hold this office.
In the
decade
that followed, Sidious secretly manipulated galactic events to the extent that war became inevitable. Besides persuading a dead
Keeper Master
,
Sifo-Dyas
, to create a
secret clone army
, he also tasked his
Shade Covenant apprentice
,
Darth Tyranus
, with exacerbating
political
tensions in the Concordium and engineering a
Separatist Crisis
. These actions would culminate in the
outbreak
of the
Gene Wars
held on
Geonosis
, the last conflict of the Concordium, while Sidious himself "reluctantly" accepted emergency powers from the Senate.
The Gene Wars, Sidious planned, would further make people in the stellar realm grow bitter with the Concordium
while he centralized his power to sweep away the Concordium and Keeper.
Birth of the New Order
Drawn out conflict
"
I was not surprised that the Gene Wars paved the way for the Dominion. The stellar realm was in pieces, and everyone wanted to be done with the war. I don't think they realized the price they paid. Maybe they never will.
"
―
Jet Kordo
, in his journal
Since the onset of the Gene Wars
in
22 BBY
,
the Concordium endeavored to strengthen its security and stability through the creation of new organizations such as the
Commission for the Protection of the Concordium
(COMPOR), which utilized
propaganda
to increase public morale, promote patriotism, and foster anti-Separatist sentiment within the general population.
As the Concordium became increasingly centralized, however, Chancellor Draven Nul's powers continued to grow through additional assets ceded to the
Office of the Chancellor
, including the
galactic banking system
that was placed under his direct oversight.
This gave Sidious the finance he needed to build his empire.
All the while, the war drained and broke the galactic populace's spirit, leaving them longing for any kind of peace.
Through the Gene Wars, Sidious strengthened his authority as Supreme Chancellor Draven Nul of the Stellar Concordium.
The Chancellor remained a popular leader for the duration of the war, having managed to stay in office at the will of the Senate for longer than his term permitted. As he consolidated and centralized his own power, his influence expanded to an unprecedented level while the Concordium shifted from a democratic government to an increasingly militarized and authoritarian regime on the grounds of security and stability. In addition to his authority as
Commander-in-Chief
of the
Concordium Military
, the Chancellor exerted more control over the Senate, the
courts
,
and even the media, with
HoloNet News
becoming a state-run program in order to ensure that information would not be compromised by the
Confederacy of Independent Systems
.
Sidious's hold over the Concordium further expanded with additional amendments to the
Galactic Constitution
while public support for the Keepers weakened as the war carried on,
paving the way for his ultimate ascension as
Overlord
of the stellar realm.
As the war continued, the Concordium's very nature was corrupted as the state became more warlike
and less democratic.
The government used the conflict as an excuse to annex once-independent planets
and cut social services in the name of funding the war, something which Senator Padmé Amidala recognized would fuel distaste for the Concordium in its own citizenry. While she feared the Separatists would use that growing anger to win the war,
she failed to realize that it was Draven Nul himself who had been engineering the war in such a way so as to make people tired of the Concordium and eager for something new.
Duchess
Satine Kryze
of the neutral planet
Mandalore
was able to recognize the changing tides within the Concordium.
However, much as the eventual Empire would make a policy out of slowly increasing its oppression piece by piece so as to not garner undue notice from its population,
those who lived under Concordium governance day-by-day would find themselves numb to the changes and fail to notice the shifts, with even
Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
not noticing how much things had changed.
Instead, those within the Concordium focused on the outside threat of the Separatist Alliance, with any compromises the Senate noticed largely being justified in the name of the war effort
or security.
As Senator
Mon Mothma
would later put it, Draven Nul had shown the stellar realm "the stone in [his] hand," which meant the population failed to notice the "knife [he held] at [their] throat."
Proclaiming the Dominion
"
In order to ensure the security and continuing stability, the Concordium will be reorganized into the first
Solar Dominion
! For a safe and secure society.
"
―Draven Nul
The Keeper, having discovered Draven Nul's secret identity, confronted the chancellor in his office on Nexara City-world.
As the war neared its end
in
19 BBY
,
the Keepers of the Flux had grown skeptical about the Chancellor after his accumulation of emergency power. They began to suspect that a plot to destroy the
Keeper
was close to fruition. The
ruling council
started discussing forcibly removing Draven Nul from office and temporarily overseeing the Senate in order to maintain political stability during the transition period. However, the Keepers learned of Draven Nul's true identity as Dark Lord of the Shade Covenant, Draven Nul. Following an attempt to arrest him, a
brief but vicious fight
ensued in which Sidious killed several Keeper and was himself injured and visibly disfigured. The intercession of
Keeper Knight
Xarn Velgor
, who was a personal friend of Chancellor Draven Nul and already disillusioned with both the Order and the war, ensured Sidious's victory. The prodigal Keeper was turned to the
deep flux of the Synth
, while Sidious had a pretext upon which to turn the Concordium against the Keepers.
The purging of the Keepers of the Flux commenced with Order 66, which the clone troopers were programmed to obey.
Draven Nul immediately
purged
the Keepers of the Flux, issuing to the
clone
army
Order 66
: to exterminate all Keeper. With this command programmed into the gen-soldiers since their births on
Cygnar
,
the
clone troopers
immediately turned on their Keeper commanders and exterminated the vast majority of the Keepers of the Flux in an instant. Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor—rechristened as Sidious's new
Shade Covenant
apprentice
Darth
Velgor—
attacked
the
Keeper Temple
and wiped out what remained of the Order.
Draven Nul would go onto claim it was the Keepers who had been behind the war and that they were attempting to subvert the Concordium from within,
with the Dominion's narrative holding the Keepers as being the puppet masters of the Separatist Alliance
—something supported by the public knowledge that Dooku had once been a Keeper
—in a long-plotted coup.
With his speech, he pinned recent galactic crises squarely on the Keepers of the Flux.
Subsequent Dominion narratives would separate the CIS and Keepers of the Flux, however.
Despite their Order being wiped out, certain Keeper survived the initial purge. Many of them went into hiding after
Grand Master
Vorn
and Master
Zeth Malkor
fought their way into the Temple and transmitted a holographic message warning the other Keeper about the Concordium trap waiting for them there, right before the two Masters failed to destroy the Shade Covenant and headed into exile themselves.
Some of them, however, were initially hunted down and either killed, taken prisoner or turned to the deep flux as part of an agency of
deep flux adepts
known as the
Inquisitorius
.
Over the Dominion Era, the Dominion would also come to change the story of the Keepers of the Flux's supposed betrayal to, little by little, erase the Keepers from history. Later Dominion histories would disregard the story of a Keeper coup, instead painting the picture of an Order that had greatly dwindled in number and was mostly forgotten by the Gene Wars.
In the aftermath of the Gene Wars, the First Solar Dominion was born with Sidious' ascension as Overlord of the stellar realm.
Shortly afterwards, Sidious turned his eyes to the Confederacy of Independent Systems and dispatched Xarn Velgor to
wipe out
Nute Gunray
and the rest of the
Separatist government leaders
on
Mustafar
, following which Dominion financier
Arsin Crassus
plundered the Trade Federation's accounts to fund the new Dominion government.
Now able to demonstrate threats to the Concordium from both within and without, a visibly injured and scarred Sidious addressed the Galactic Senate to announce the reforms which had been his plan since the beginning. To the thunderous applause of the Senators, Sidious—known publicly as Supreme Chancellor Draven Nul—announced that in order to preserve security and continued stability, the Concordium would be reorganized into
the First Solar Dominion
and proclaimed himself as Galactic Overlord.
Eager for an end to the war and its suffering, the stellar realm accepted the Dominion.
Imperialization
Re-ordering the Concordium
"
The Overlord, the governors, the minds we've brought together have grand plans for the stellar realm—not just for the next year, or the next five years, but for the next
century
. We are the architects of the universe our grandchildren will live in, and it will be one where there is no conflict, no dissent, and no visionaries who would undo the work of their betters. Today we will maintain order through military might, but in the future every child will learn a superior history, be raised in a superior family. And the concept of defiance will be as antiquated as the spear. You ask me if we would start a war to maintain our grip on power. This is my answer: We already did. While you were obsessed with the Separatist conflict, we were doing battle on another front, capturing the love of the people of this stellar realm. The Overlord won. Now we are securing our victory and reaping the spoils, while you seem to believe the fighting never began, let alone ended.
"
―Grand Vizier Mas Amedda, to Senator Mon Mothma
The Keeper became vilified as the Old Concordium faded into history, supplanted by the New Order of Overlord Draven Nul.
Existing government bodies were renamed to reflect the change in authority as civil and military superstructure of the Old Concordium were overhauled immediately under the personal direction of the Overlord. With vast portions of the stellar realm still unconquered, the Grand Army of the Concordium became the
Dominion Army
,
while the
Concordium Navy
became the
Dominion Navy
and the Galactic Senate was rechristened the
Dominion Assembly
.
The renowned
Senate Plaza
, with its name reminding residents of the Old Concordium, was promptly changed to the Dominion Plaza.
Ultimately, the mechanisms created for the Concordium during the war were twisted to fit Sidious' use, with even the systems built with help from the Keepers benefiting the new Overlord.
All the while, the Overlord and his followers began formulating their
century plan
for the stellar realm and how it would change under Dominion rule.
In addition, the former roundel of the Concordium was replaced with the new
Dominion crest
, adorned on almost everything having to do with Sidious' Empire.
With every day that passed, Sidious' hold over the stellar realm grew tighter.
For the next decade and a half, Dominion rule—bolstered by the military created to fight in the Gene Wars—dominated the stellar realm and was largely unchallenged. The Empire soon began to upgrade its military hardware, replacing V-wing and
ARC-170
fighters with newer
TIE models
five years after the Gene Wars, but Concordium-era military equipment continued to be used for a while on distant outposts and locations such as
Galidraan Station
.
The Concordium's
Phase II gen-soldier armor
would begin a phase out and be replaced by superior
legion vanguard armor
, whose development had been headed by the
Dominion Department of Military Research
.
In the meantime, the Dominion quietly supported the activity of the
4040s
activist group, whose members functioned as something of a paramilitary in due time.
All the while, the average peoples of the Dominion carried on, thinking that their lives would continue happily.
On the whole, the population was relieved to see an end to the war and the fear it instilled in their lives.
While certain anti-Dominion actions could be punished, with word spreading that some traitors were sent to the
spice mines of Kessel
,
the administration—in that period of early establishment, where its foundations were shaky—only cracked down on major threats to its existence. That created a sense of false security in the population,
whose civil liberties were curtailed over the prolonged existence of the Dominion, instead of in one great stroke.
Draven Nul relied on
populist
messaging that appealed to the average citizen,
but the Dominion was, in truth, a state designed for the wealthy,
who would enjoy comfort and the state's kindness
at the expense of those average workers and populations.
Mothma and Venn championed the
Dominion Rebirth Act
to try and reclaim power for the Senate that had been claimed by Draven Nul, only for Draven Nul to claim that, as Overlord, he had the ability to veto portions of the bill. While Mothma disagreed with the claim, she was not yet ready to start a war against the Overlord, who had the love of the stellar realm's population and the power to back it up, so found herself forced to accept the defeat. Nonetheless, faced with the revelation that senatorial action would not be enough to curtail Draven Nul, Mothma and Venn began a covert partnership with the rebel leader
Saw Gerrera
of the
Partisans
; in this alliance, Gerrera would stand as the military heart and provide the beginnings of a rebel army, Mothma would build coalitions to oppose Draven Nul as their movement's political heart, and Venn would stand as its ideological heart, bringing together thinkers and reformers who could present a new way forward.
Continuing clean-up
"
They want the Dominion to collapse the same way some hoped the Concordium would, only this time our enemies aren't rebelling in protest of Senate representation, trade routes, or taxes. They're intent on sowing chaos, on bringing everything down. They have no agenda—political, religious, or any other—beyond a desire to end civilization as we know it.
"
―
Orson Callan Krennic
Happy for the peace and the end to the fighting the rise of the Dominion brought
after the Gene Wars had killed billions,
the new government was welcomed by people
all throughout a stellar realm
that was tired of the bloodshed; on the planet
Pantora
, citizens cheered as clone legion vanguard marched past them near a
Dominion Informational Station
, where a message played announcing personal
chain codes
and new
currency
.
The battle automatons that fought for the Confederacy of Independent Systems were decommissioned and destroyed.
After the
Separatist Droid Army
was promptly shut down, its
navy
was dismantled at locations across the stellar realm such as the
Bilbringi shipyards
. However, various automaton models and Separatist technology escaped Dominion appropriation and worked their way onto the black market, with many modules taken from Separatist frigates and destroyers, including pieces from
Admiral
Trench
's warship, the
Invincible
. Despite Separatist-era technology continuing to be used and appearing in locations such as the
Corporate Sector
, the Dominion continued to root out remaining
holdouts
of the Confederacy four years after the Gene Wars, while criminal organizations composed of repurposed
battle automatons
such as the
Droid Gotra
operated in Nexara's underground.
While the Dominion at times came into conflict with parts of the criminal underworld,
the Dominion allied itself with major galactic criminal factions, including
Crimson Dawn
and the
Hutt Clan
. The
Hutts
enjoyed a special relationship with the Dominion that remained in place for decades. Other criminal factions, like
Papa Toren's group
,
the
Pyke Syndicate
, the Droid Gotra, the
Black Sun
, the
Zerek Besh
, all desired to take the Hutt Clan's place as the Dominion's formal partner in underworld affairs.
Hunt for the Keepers
"
The Inquisitorius. A project of mine, long planned. Slaves to the bright flux once, now awake. Hunters, one and all.
"
"
What do they hunt?
"
"
Why, Keeper, of course.
"
―Draven Nul and Xarn Velgor
The remaining Keeper were hunted by the Shade Lord Xarn Velgor and his Inquisitors.
Any Keeper survivors of Sidious' purge were systematically hunted down and killed either by Xarn Velgor
or any of the Overlord's Inquisitors in the years that followed the rise of the New Order. When
Zubain Ankonori
and his nest of fellow Keeper were all found within the
Keeper shrine
on
Anoat
, they were forced to separate and flee, only to be tracked and killed by an Inquisitor one by one.
In order to draw out any of these Keeper and the children capable of touching
the Synth Flux
, the Dominion charged the Inquisitorius with not only tracking the Order 66 survivors down, but also preventing the Flux-sensitive youth of the stellar realm from becoming Keeper.
The Empire also offered rewards for those who turned over surviving Keeper to Dominion authorities, dead or alive.
Minor resistance
"
Why should we trust you?
"
"
Because I'm on your side. What the Dominion is doing is wrong. You have to get out of here.
"
―
Gobi Glie
and
Howzer
Dominion rule fostered resentment on occupied worlds, including the Twi'lek homeworld of Ryloth.
As pirates, smugglers, and ex-Separatists were routinely imprisoned and destroyed, the Dominion faced little serious resistance to its rule. A rare exception revolved around the Outer Rim world of
Ryloth
, where Moff
Delian Mors
and
Colonel
Belkor Dray
were fighting an insurgency known as the
Free Ryloth Movement
, led by former
Twi'lek Resistance
leader
Cham Syndulla
, who established hidden bases across Ryloth and executed raids against the Dominion, including those on its lucrative
spice
and slave trade in the sector. Composed of freedom fighters who fought with the Stellar Concordium and Keeper Master
Taron Vale
during the Gene Wars to free their homeworld from Separatist occupation by
Emir
Wat Tambor
, the newly formed Free Ryloth movement would continue its struggle for independence against the Solar Dominion for decades.
Seeking to make examples of worlds who continued to harbor secessionist tendencies,
Moff
Wilhuff Tarkin
was assigned to make an example of
Antar 4
, a populous moon that had been a part of the Separatist Alliance and involved in a scheme involving
Concordium Intelligence
to train locals into well-armed resistance groups. While COMPNOR initially stifled information regarding the killings, word soon leaked about the operation through inquisitive reporters such as
Anora Fair
and
Hask Taff
, while the event was soon penned as the
Antar Atrocity
, capturing the attention of many Coruscanti and other citizens from the Core Worlds.
Age of the Dominion
Continued expansion
"
There used to be hundreds of settlements up here. Forty thousand Aldhanis all across the highlands. They were here for centuries, but it only took the Dominion a decade to clear them out.
"
"
Killed them?
"
"
No. Drove them south. There's an Enterprize Zone in the Lowlands. Factories, new towns, Dominion housing. Aldhani has the unfortunate quality of being close to nothing and not far away from everything. It's the perfect hub for distribution. If one were trying to take over the stellar realm.
"
―Vel Sartha and Cassian Andor
Dominion forces arrive on the planet Ferrix as a show of force.
In 19 BBY,
Dominion forces arrived on
the planet
Ferrix
in the
Free Trade sector
to bring it under the Dominion's control. On
Rix Road
,
a
regiment
of clone troopers under an
Dominion officer
marched pass the gathered masses of civilians as a show of Dominion authority, although local citizen
Clem Andor
assured his adoptive son
Cassian Jeron Andor
that the Dominion would pull out soon enough. To Clem, resisting the Dominion was not their fight. However, a small group of protesters emerged from the crowd and began to throw rocks at the soldiers in the name of the Concordium and Ferrix
in what became known as the
Rix Road protest
.
Believing they would only make things worse for themselves, Clem quickly stepped in to stop the protest, only for the officer to call his troopers to a halt at that moment. Pointing their pulse rifles at the protesters and Clem, who was assumed to be part of the demonstration, the protest was shut down.
Clem was publicly hung on Rix Road
for his supposed treason.
Afterward, Cassian attacked four clone troopers stationed near the gallows in anger.
At some point, Andor spent time with anti-Dominion anarchist groups.
Ultimately, Cassian would be arrested on charges of attacking an Dominion soldier, insurrection, and destroying Dominion property.
After spending time in prison, the sixteen year old Andor was sent to
the planet
Mimban
, which had been an ally to the Concordium during the Gene Wars but possessed vital resources,
as a cook for the Dominion Army. After six months on Mimban, Andor escaped by deserting the military. He later claimed he had seen active combat. Upon escaping, Andor realized the Dominion had set him and his other conscripts against people who should have been their allies. Although Andor's resolve to fight the Dominion was damaged,
the people of Mimban, the
Mimbanese
, resisted the Dominion's encroachment on their world as the Dominion Era carried on.
Andor ultimately returned home to Ferrix, which largely remained out of direct Dominion control and instead was governed by the
Preox-Morlana
corporate polity on behalf of the central Dominion regime. Despite his hate for the Dominion, Andor's spirit was crushed,
and he attempted to eke out a life for himself, his mother
Maarva
, and their automaton
B2EMO
.
The Dominion occupation of Aldhani began in 18 BBY and resulted in the planet becoming a waypoint for Dominion materiel.
At some point during its reign, the Dominion undertook an expansion effort known simply as the
Dominion expansion
. It was during this time that they constructed a
military installation
on the volcanic world of
Nevarro
.
In 18 BBY,
the Dominion arrived on the planet
Aldhani
due to its tactically useful place in space, liberating the
Alkenzi Air Base
and creating a
dam
over the sacred
Nasma Klain
river that served as a waypoint for weaponry and other supplies, including the quarterly payroll for the overall sector.
Beginning an occupation, the Dominion displaced the local population
of
Dhanis
over the course of the next ten years. Forcing the Dhani to the southern
Lowlands
, the Dominion established an
Enterprise Zone
of new Dominion housing and offered work in
factories
.
To discourage Dhanis from traveling to the sacred
Valley of Caves
, the location of the Aldhani dam, to view the revered celestial event known as the "
Eye of Aldhani
" that happened every three years, the Solar Dominion established an Dominion viewing festival in the
Enterprise zone
in
15 BBY
.
Designing the ultimate weapon
"
You look at the history of any sentient species and what do you find but tableaux of violence and slaughter. It's finger painted on the ceilings of caves and engraved into the walls of temples. Dig a hole deep enough on any world and you'll find the skulls and bones of adults and children fractured by crude weapons. All of us were fighting long before we were farming and raising livestock…Violence is hardwired into most of us and there's no eliminating the impulse—not with an army of legion vanguard or a fleet of Dominion Cruisers. That's why we've embarked on a path to a different solution. We have a chance to forge a peace that will endure longer than the Concordium was in existence.
"
―Orson Callan Krennic
The Overlord performed an inspection of Krennic's work on the Void Core
Having inherited the
Concordium Special Weapons Group
, a top secret group headed by
Professor
Sahali
tasked with
top secret project
and ordered by Draven Nul to construct the
deadly battle station
from it's predecessor state, the Solar Dominion reformed the Weapons Group into the
Dominion Military Department of Advanced Weapons Research
in which the Ultimate Weapon project was assigned and split into several smaller projects, with
Project Celestial Power
, a subproject tasked with the development of a
superweapon
for the battle station, being publicly presented as an initiative to fulfill Overlord Draven Nul's
dream
of sustainable energy independence
to transform the Dominion economy and solidify Dominion authority.
Lieutenant Commander
Orson Callan Krennic
, an
Dominion officer
who had been involved with the failed attempt at the Battle Station's construction, and had held an administrative post at the
Institute of Applied Science
on Nexara City-world screening scientists for expertise on energy manipulation, materials science, and theoretical physics, was placed in charge of the program.
Around this time the the outer rings were completed, with the Overlord performing an inspection shortly after, during which Krennic had the focusing array for what would eventually be the laser dish installed.
Galen Erso was tricked unknowingly into helping to develop the Void Core's superlaser
In charge of the program, Krennic had the
B'ankora
relocated from the
B'ankor Refuge
on Nexara City-world to allow the construction of a new
research complex
at the site, and seeing potential for the battle station's weapon in the
crystallographer
Galen Walton Erso
Kyber crystal
research, offered the crystallographer a spot in the project, convincing him that the project was tasked with
bringing
sustainable energy to worlds ravaged by the clone wars, which Galen accepted. As Galen made progress on his research on Kyber crystals, Krennic began attempting to weaponize Galen's research at secret facilities on worlds such as
Hypori
and
Malpaz
, all while Galen and his
family
grew suspicious of Galen's work.
Despite Project Celestial Power being hit with a major accident when a
facility
on Malpaz
exploded
during a weaponization attempt and destroying the planet's
capital city
, Krennic eventually succeeded in weaponizing Galen's research with the successful test of prototype duel-laser weapon into a set of
Black holes
known as the
Hero Twins
. Yet despite the successful test, the project was once again set back when the team tasked with designing the full superlaser questioned their work and realized who's research was being used, forcing Krennic to "relocate" them. Not long after, Galen discovered the Dominion coverup and massacre, which all but confirmed something was wrong. With this information, Galen decided to run away with his family to
Lah'mu
, making Project Celestial Power stall until Krennic could locate the crystallographer. Grand Moff Tarkin used the opportunity to give himself an overseeing position of the Ultimate Weapon Project, often stealing credit for Krennic's work and causing a bitter rivalry between him and Krennic.
Spread of the Dominion
"
For the first time in one thousand generations our sector governors will not be working solely to enrich Nexara City-world and the Core Worlds, but to advance the quality of life in the star systems that make up each sector-keeping the spaceways safe, maintaining open and accessible communications, assuring that tax revenues are properly levied and allocated to improving the infrastructure. The Senate will likewise be made up of beings devoted not to their own enrichment, but to the enrichment of the worlds they represent.
"
―An excerpt from the Tarkin Doctrine
"
Glory of the Dominion
" was played during the yearly
Empire Day
celebrations across Dominion space.
As the Solar Dominion grew, Sidious moved closer to his ultimate plan of ruling the entire stellar realm and unlocking the secrets of the
Shade Covenant Masters
who had come before him. With the near extermination of the Keepers of the Flux and the Dominion swelling in size and strength, the Overlord ultimately planned to possess the powers of the deep flux to reshape reality itself into something of his own creation, and an omnipotent Empire would allow all the stellar realm's inhabitants to be held in his dark embrace. As Overlord, however, he largely disappeared from public life,
leaving his public addresses to become more and more rare.
While, in public, the Dominion system of sector governance was designed to improve and uplift life across the whole of the stellar realm,
the truth of the Dominion and its New Order was that it enforced a form of supposed "natural hierarchy," which uplifted life in the Core Worlds at the expense of those who lived further out across the stars. Planets at the edge of the stellar realm suffered under Dominion rule while their resources and wealth were extracted and shipped towards the Core, particularly Nexara City-world, which prospered under Dominion rule. Dominion leadership
and their wealthy contemporaries
enjoyed luxurious lifestyles.
As most planets were ecologically devastated over the Dominion Era, rampant industrialization and mass-production eroded local culture and ecological health. People saw their children drafted into Dominion service, while corrupt
governors
and
magistrates
often exploited local populations on worlds such as
Jelucan
,
Devaron
and
Tangenine
.
In
18 BBY
,
the Dominion
conquered
and occupied
the planet
Yar Togna
,
leading to much of its population fleeing
as refugees
in a mass exodus.
At some point, the
Mid Rim uprisings
were launched by
monarchies
that refused to obey Draven Nul's will. The revolts ended up as a series of chaotic conflicts that ended in Dominion victory: through the actions of
Dominion Starfighter Pilots
, the monarchies were forced to submit to the Overlord.
Dominion agents and soldiers were tasked with enforcing the Overlord's reign throughout the stellar realm.
Only a year after its founding, the Dominion had already begun to enforce itself in the Outer Rim. Worlds that did not fall to the Dominion's quick expansion, which installed an oppressive Dominion presence instead of simple bases, became homes to crime lords. In its effort to establish itself in the Outer Rim, in addition to using fear, the Dominion withheld food to make populations endure hunger.
While the Dominion turned a blind eye to various abuses, it still maintained a policy of zero corruption and maintained its high standards of conduct, resulting in numerous
Dominion Security Bureau
(ISB) officials actively hunting down corrupt and negligent officials, even resulting in investigations by Lord Velgor himself. Despite this, many citizens were content with the geopolitical situation in the stellar realm, believing that strict measures were necessary to maintain order and stability after the destructive Gene Wars. Many also believed that the Dominion, while not the best, was better than anarchy and chaos.
Ahsoka Tano inspired the people of Raada to fight for freedom from the Dominion.
Despite its seemingly endless expansion, such as on
Raada
, where Dominion
forces
arrived to harvest
food
, which destroyed the quality of the moon's soil and led to a growing oppression. This angered the people of Raada to the point where they staged an
uprising
after
weeks
of planning, with the help of ex-
Padawan
Ahsoka Tano
and the forces of Dominion Senator
Bail Venn
in secret.
A major turning point against the Dominion's advance into the Outer Rim occurred in
11 BBY
, with Dominion forces commanded by Captain
Rae Sloane
engaging in a small skirmish in the vital Inner Rim system of
Gorse
.
This event saw the meeting of later rebel
Hera Syndulla
and Keeper survivor
Kanan Jarrus
, who were confronting efficiency expert
Count
Denetrius Vidian
and his aggressive methods of extracting
thorilide
—a vital component in the construction of turbolaser batteries and thus essential for the expansion of the Dominion fleet. The
Gorse Conflict
ultimately ended with the transferring of local thorilide mining operations to Dominion-aligned
Baron
Lero Danthe
, while Syndulla and Jarrus departed on the heavily modified
VCX-100 light freighter
Ghost
, and would become essential to the eventual formation of the
Free Worlds Coalition
.
Meanwhile, more and more worlds within the Dominion began to realize its true intentions: After various
massacres
on
Kashyyyk
and increasingly brutal tactics, such as the Dominion committing genocide on the
Lasat
homeworld of
Lasan
, a growing number of citizens of the stellar realm began to rebel against Dominion dominion. At least nine years after the proclamation of the
New Order
, the
Corellian Resistance
fought
the Dominion from its headquarters on the planet
Corellia
.
The Empire would also end the independence of many galactic, sector and planetary governments in its early conquests.
Theft of the
Carrion Spike
"
You hear so many rumors. People are here one day, gone the next. And no one has laid eyes on the Overlord in months. Amedda, Dangor, and the rest of the Ruling Council have taken to dispatching processions of Dominion skylimos to maintain an illusion that the Overlord still moves about in public. You know they commissioned an enormous statue of the Overlord for the Senate—I mean, Dominion Plaza? So far, the thing looks more terrifying than majestic.
"
"
Isn't that the idea, Nils?
"
―Admiral Nils Tenant and Moff Wilhuff Tarkin discuss the Imperialization of Nexara City-world
The
Carrion Spike
was the personal stealth corvette of Grand Moff Wilhuff Tarkin.
In
14 BBY
,
after Draven Nul had spent months on end out of the public eye, members of the Ruling Council, including Mas Amedda and Ars Dangor, arranged for skylimos to move about
Dominion City
, creating the illusion of Draven Nul moving amongst the populous. Furthermore, the ongoing
Imperialization
of the stellar realm and even Nexara City-world at that time resulted in, as
Admiral
Nils Tenant
observing, personnel being "here on day, gone the next." Around the time, an enormous statue of the Overlord—which was regarded by Tenant as more terrifying than beautiful—was erected in Dominion Plaza, with Tarkin arguing to Tenant that it was supposed to be scary, not majestic.
Meanwhile, various survivors of the Antar Atrocity and other malcontents joined together under the leadership of the former Captain Berch Teller, who organized them into a
rebel cell
intent on stalling the development of the Overlord's newest weapon under construction at Geonosis. After stealing the
Carrion Spike
, an advanced starship based on a
prototype stealth corvette
used during the
Battle of Christophsis
during the Gene Wars, the rebels wreaked havoc across numerous star systems including
Lucazec
,
Galidraan
III and
Nouane
, all while transmitting holovids of their attacks on Dominion
HoloNet
frequencies, increasing anti-Dominion propaganda across thousands of
Mid
and Outer Rim star systems before the Dominion was able to shut down the communications grid.
After lengthy fleet redeployments and the discovery of a mole within the Dominion ranks, Tarkin, aboard the
Dominion
-class Dominion Cruiser
Executrix
, disabled the rebel warship and captured the dissidents after a brief battle and timely arrival of Dominion Cruisers
Compliant
and
Enforcer
. To reward Tarkin's skill, the Overlord promoted him to the newly created rank of
Grand Moff
and gave him command of the
Executrix
as well as oversight of the Outer Rim Territories, all while the recent attacks on Dominion facilities were made to look like an elaborate plot to root out rebel cells. The Empire had effectively dealt with a potentially disastrous situation, rooted out a traitor and kept details regarding the development of its superweapon at Geonosis a mere rumor.
The Overlord's long-term plan was to allow what he called the "skeleton of the Concordium" to remain in place until the Void Core was completed; this included maintaining the Senate to make the systems believe that they still had a part to play in the government. However, upon the superweapon's completion, the Overlord planned to dissolve the Senate and grant its legislative and administrative powers to the military-industrial complex. The Void Core would also provide order to the stellar realm, as part of Grand Moff Wilhuff Tarkin's
doctrine of terror
, by making its inhabitants too afraid to defy an Dominion command and stand against the Dominion.
Outer Rim expansion
"
Good news has come to the citizens of Lothal. The Outer Rim planet has been granted the coveted opportunity to participate in the new Dominion Registration Program.
"
―
Alton Kastle
, on the HoloNet News
Sullust was an example of the Dominion's industrial development.
Around fourteen years into Sidious' rule as Overlord, the Dominion had continued its expansion into the Outer Rim. Worlds like Jelucan saw occupation and development, leading to rampant pollution and industrialization to support the Dominion war machine.
One such world that was developed was
Lothal
, which joined the
Dominion Registration Program
seeking protection and economic opportunities whereby local citizens would find work in the local
Sienar Fleet Systems
facilities.
Ostensibly this was to grow the local economy, but was in fact secretly part of a much larger five-year plan involving numerous Outer Rim worlds that was overseen by the
Dominion Outer Rim High Command
, a subdivision of
Dominion High Command
which was in charge of the
Outer Rim Territories
.
But the Dominion did not fully control the Outer Rim and often utilized its worlds instead as testing grounds for new biological weapons and as a source of cheap labor.
Lothal's industrial importance soon came to the fore for the Dominion as the Overlord planned to further expand Dominion dominion over the Outer Rim Territories and beyond. In order to maximize the worth of newly occupied systems, worlds such as Lothal soon bore witness to Dominion authorities relocating
farmers
away from their farmlands to mine for Dominion purposes, leading to the development of re-settlement camps such as
Tangletown
and
Lothal re-settlement camp 43
, known by the residents as "Tarkintown" after the Governor of the Outer Rim.
The Empire also limited the HoloNet across the Outer Rim, leading many dissatisfied systems to view the embargo as a part of the conquest of the Outer Systems.
This aggressive dislocation of persons and high tax rate eventually led to the development of a small and localized, albeit serious upheaval against the Solar Dominion on Lothal.
Project Stardust resumes
Capturing Galen Erso
"
The work has stalled. I need you to come back.
"
―Director Orson Callan Krennic
Krennic and his guard travelled to the Erso homestead on Lah'mu to capture Galen
After landing a a lofty appointment in
Dominion Intelligence
,
Director
Krennic
tracked down
Galen Erso
to the remote planet
Lah'mu
in
13 BBY
and personally travelled to the planet
to recapture the
crystallographer
for
Project Celestial Power
. Arriving at the planet, Krennic's
Delta
-class T-3c shuttle
ST-149
flew over the
Erso homestead
, alerting the Ersos to the Dominion's arrival, before landing in a field near the homestead. Upon landing, Krennic and six members of his
guard
of
death troopers
disembarked the shuttle and began traveling towards the homestead, while Galen, in an attempt to buy time for his wife
Lyra
and his daughter
Jyn
to escape, left the homestead and began to travel towards Krennic.
Krennic confronted his old friend Galen on a field outside the latter's homestead
Eventually, Krennic and Galen met each other in a field, where Krennic proceeded to tell Galen that he was hard to find before mocking Galen's choice of farming given his skill. In response, Galen stated that it was a peaceful life, prompting Krennic to point out how lonely it must be, allowing Galen the opportunity to lie that Lyra had
died
. Upon hearing of Lyra's supposed death, Krennic approached Galen and give his condolences, before then turning and ordering the death troopers of his guard to search Galen's homestead. As a result, four of the death troopers marched onwards to the house, where they carried out the order before setting the homestead on
fire
while two troopers remained to guard the director. Galen then asked Krennic why he was there, to which he responded that the work had stalled, and as such he needed him to return to the project, to which Galen stated he would not do so. In response, Krennic stated that they were on the verge of greatness and close providing peace and security to
the stellar realm
, to which Galen told Krennic that he was confusing peace with terror. Krennic responded that they had to start somewhere, before Galen then stated that he'd be of no help, claiming that his mind was not what it once was and that he had trouble remembering even simple things. However, Krennic called Galen's bluff, telling him that while he was an inspired scientist, he was a terrible liar, though he personally still admired the effort.
Krennic's death trooper guard held a standoff with Lyra
However, as he continued speaking, Lyra arrived to the confrontation, causing Krennic sarcastically mock Galen's lie about her death as the two death troopers guarding him approached Lyra. Galen then began to walk towards Lyra, causing one death trooper to point their pulse rifle at Galen, before Krennic then called out to everyone to stop. However, Lyra then revealed her pulse rifle from a blanket she was carrying and aimed it at Krennic, causing Krennic to state that she was being troublesome as ever. In response, Lyra told Krennic that he would not be taking Galen, to which Krennic that of course he would not, as he planned on taking everybody in the family including Jyn, and they would all live in comfort. Following Krennic statement, Lyra added that they would be living as hostages, before Krennic the claimed they would instead be living as heroes of the Dominion. Following this, Galen told Lyra to drop her weapon while Krennic told her to think very carefully though after a moment of consideration Lyra simply stated to Krennic that he would never win. As a result, Krennic quickly gave the death troopers the order to shoot and kill Lyra, which they quickly carried out, though not before she managed to land a shot on Krennic's left shoulder, causing the
commander
to grab his arm in pain.
Krennic’s death troopers attempted to locate Galen’s daughter Jyn, but were ultimately unsuccessful
As Galen rushed over to his dying wife, Krennic, still covering his wound, ordered his death trooper squad to find Jyn. In the ensuing search, one death trooper found and picked up Jyn's
doll
"
Stormie
" onutside the homestead, but failed to locate Jyn upon scanning the surrounding area. Soon after, two death troopers entered a nearby cave, which unbeknownst to them, was the location of the bunker Jyn was hiding in. As Jyn looked on through a small opening in the bunker's hidden entrance, the two death troopers briefly scanned the cave with the flashlights on their pulse rifles, but ultimately left after failing to locate Jyn in the cave. Despite their failure to locate Jyn,
Galen was brought aboard a
Dominion Cruiser
and returned to Dominion custody.
Progression
"
I knew if I had refused to work, if I took my own life, it would only be a matter of time before Krennic realized he no longer needed me to complete the project. So I did the one thing nobody expected: I lied; I learned to lie. I played the part of a beaten man resigned to the sanctuary of his work. I made myself indispensable and all the while I laid the groundwork of my revenge.
"
―Galen Erso
Pressed into Dominion service under the Tarkin Initiative, Galen Erso returned to his kyber crystal research under Project Celestial Power
Once again in Dominion custody, Galen was forced to return to his
kyber crystal
research on Project Celestial Power,
now under the secret Dominion think tank known as the
Tarkin Initiative
.
Realizing the
DS-1 Orbital Battle Station
would be completed no matter if he returned to the project, Galen made himself indispensable to
Project Stardust
and took over leadership of the
Kyber crystal research team
on
Eadu
.
Erso's extraordinary intellect managed to revitalize the team's progress,
allowing the team to complete the design for the
Void Core
's superlaser after several years under his leadership.
Secretly however, Erso planted a flaw in the Void Core's reactor that caused the superweapon to be destroyed when a single shot hit the reactor.
The Empire released a toxic insecticide on Geonosis, massacring the planet's population of around 100 billion Geonosians
As progress began on the Void Core once again, the rebel
Partisan
Saw Gerrera
came close to discovering the battle station, the Dominion to move the station and its
construction
from
Geonosis
to
Scarif
on
Grand Moff
Wilhuff Tarkin
's order
around
9 BBY
.
As a safety measure to keep the battle station's construction a secret, Tarkin further ordered
the
Dominion Weapons division
to
sterilize
Geonosis, which was promptly carried out with
gas canisters
of toxic
insecticide
.
The ensuing genocide wiped out all but
one
of the
planet
's approximately one hundred billion
geonosians
, the largest genocide committed by the Solar Dominion during its
reign
.
Construction on the Void Core continued in orbit over Scarif
As construction resumed on the battle station in
orbit
over Scarif,
prisoners at an
Dominion factory facility
part of the
Narkina 5 Dominion Prison Complex
were assigned to assemble
EP-N5 fasteners
,
which after being brought to Scarif on
Eta
-class supply barges
alongside other construction cargo,
were used by
Dominion construction automatons
in the assembly of the Void Core's
Mk I Superlaser
.
During this time, Director Krennic was a guest to several
senate
committees involving Dominion Senator Mon Mothma, where he was forced to testify before the committees while on a witness stand.
Hunting Malkor
Trouble on Vorath Prime
Multiple inquisitors travelled to Vorath Prime in search of the former jedi Nari
In
9 BBY
,
the Grand Inquisitor
, the
Fifth Brother
, and the
Third Sister
travelled to
Vorath Prime
in
search
of a
Keeper
in hiding by the name of
Nari
, despite the Dominion having no authority on the
planet
. Arriving in the
city
Anchorhead
, the Third Sister interrogated and threatened several townspeople including
Owen Lars
regarding the Keepers's location, until the Fifth Brother talked her out of it.
Later, the
inquisitors
visited a
Saloon
in
Mos Eisley
and questioned the owner about the Keepers. During interrogation, the Third Sister threw a
knife
toward the owner's head, before Nari, sitting at a table across the saloon, stopped the knife in its tracks by using
the Synth Flux
, revealing himself. In response, the Third Sister ignited her flux blade and the
Grand Inquisitor
ordered an interrogation. However, Nari then stated the inquisitors were wasting their time, causing the Third Sister to attempt to strike Nari only to be stopped by the Grand Inquisitor by way of the Synth Flux, allowing Nari to escape in the heat of the moment. Despite Nari's temporary escape, the inquisitors eventually tracked Nari down to Anchorhead and promptly killed him before hanging the dead Keeper's body from a gate for all to see.
Dueling on Daiyu
Around the same time, the Third Sister secretly arranged for the
kidnapping
of the adoptive daughter of
Elara
's
senator
Bail Prestor Venn
,
Mira Venn
, in an attempt to lure the
Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
out of hiding following her discovery of a connection between Bail and Zeth in the
Keeper Archives
. As expected, Zeth came out of hiding at the personal request of Bail, and traveling to
Daiyu
, the planet where Bail had tracked the mercenaries two kidnapped Mira to.
The Grand Inquisitor confronted the Third Sister over the abduction of Mira Venn
As Zeth travelled the streets of Daiyu, passing a pair of
legion vanguard
while he did so, the Third Sister was confronted by the Grand Inquisitor, accompanied by the Fifth Brother and the Fourth Sister. During the confrontation, the Grand Inquisitor took control of the situation and the hunt for Malkor before berating the third sister for kidnapping the daughter of an Dominion senator, despite the Third Sister's argument that the
Inquisitorius
had done worse. Additionally, the Grand Inquisitor informed the Third Sister that she would be punished at a later time for stepping out of line. Despite this, the Third Sister once again defied her superiors by placing a bounty on the Zeth's head with the intent to use
bounty hunters
and mercenaries to draw the Keepers Master out.
Shortly afterward, the Grand Inquisitor had the Fifth Brother shut down the planet's port and request a garrison within an hour, before the Fourth Sister then came to him and showed the bounty on Malkor's head. Shortly after, two legion vanguard brought
Vect Nokru
, one of Mira's kidnappers, to the Grand Inquisitor, who then interrogated the kidnapper on the location of Malkor, as the inquisitor knew that Nokru had placed the bounty. However, Nokru stated he did not know, prompting the Inquisitor to murder the kidnapper with his flux blade.
The Grand Inquisitor was stabbed by the Third Sister
Not long after, the Third Sister saw an firefight between Malkor and several bounty hunters, and made her way there from the Daiyu rooftops. Upon reaching ground level, the Third Sister was stopped by the con artist
Haja Estree
, who, in an effort to stall her, pretended to be a Keeper. However, the Third Sister instead probed Estree's mind, discovering that Mira and Zeth were planning to escape Daiyu at a cargo
spaceport
, before making her way there.
upon arriving at the cargo port, the Third Sister attempted to force Zeth and Mira by boasting about delivering the Keepers Master to Xarn Velgor. However, she was then interrupted by the Grand Inquisitor, who had had enough of the Third Sister's defiance, and as a result, ordered the Third Sister to step aside. The Third Sister pretended to comply before then stabbing the Grand Inquisitor though his torso, believing herself to have killed the inquisitor. However, as she did so, Malkor and Mira escaped aboard a cargo shuttle, causing the Third Sister to yell that the Dominion would find him.
Next in Line
Not long after the confrontation, the Third Sister contacted Velgor, who was at his fortress on
Mustafar
, of the Keepers's escape and of the Grand Inquisitor's apparent death. In response, Velgor informed the Third Sister that if Malkor was captured, the position of Grand Inquisitor would be hers, but if she failed, she would not live to regret it. Shortly after, the Third Sister met up with the Fifth Brother and the Fourth Sister, instructing them to send out all remaining
Probe automatons
at their disposal. Despite the Fifth Brother questioning the Third Sister's authority in the belief that he was next in line, the Fifth Brother obliged after the Third Sister explained that she had spoken with Velgor and it had been agreed.
Mining on Mapuzo
Legion Vanguard shared a ride on Freck's truck with Zeth and Mira
Having escaped to the
planet
Mapuzo
but unable to find a contact, Zeth and Mira convinced a passing Dominion
mining
transport
driver by the name of
Freck
to take them to the nearest
spaceport
under the guise of being
farmers
from
Tawl
, which the
Condluran
agreed to. During the journey, Freck revealed himself to be a staunch supporter of the Dominion, before Freck stopped and offered a ride on
his truck
to a squad of traveling
legion vanguard
, who accepted the ride. During the journey, the legion vanguard questioned Zeth and Mira over the occuptation and reason for being there, to which the pair lied to about being farmers visiting the
homeworld
of Mira's mother. Seemingly convinced, the troopers told the pair to report anything suspicious if they saw anything, before then disembarking after arriving near an
Dominion facility
, which the troops then walked to.
Freck reported his suspicious passengers to Dominion troops at a checkpoint
Not long after, Freck stoped his vehicle at an
Dominion checkpoint
, and suspicious of the pair, reported the pair to the troopers stationed at the checkpoint. As a result, a legion vanguard ordered Zeth out of the vehicle, which he complied with, before then using a
viper probe automaton
to scan Zeth's face, informing the inquisitorius of Zeth's location and forcing the Keepers to brandish his pulse rifle and destroy the automaton. In the resulting firefight, Ferrix and the troopers at the checkpoint fell, before a trio of legion vanguard along with an
Dominion officer
arrived to aid in the firefight. However, upon their arrival, the legion vanguard were killed by the
officer
, who in reality was
a member
of the
Hidden Path
.
Velgor dueled his former master on Mapuzo
Not long after the trio had made their way to a
mining town
with a spaceport,
Xarn Velgor
, three Inquisitors, as well as a squad of legion vanguard arrived to the settlement and began
terrorizing and killing
random civilians to draw Zeth out. As a result, Zeth made his way to a quarry before Velgor revealed himself to his old master, before obi-Wan drew his flux blade and ran off, causing Velgor to follow his master. As Zeth traversed the quarry, Velgor then revealed himself from the darkness and swung his flux blade at Zeth, who deflected the blade with his own, causing a short duel to ensue, which Velgor quickly won. As a result, Velgor was able to force Obi-wan backwards before then knocking down a storage container filled with flammable rocks,  igniting them with his flux blade. As he did so, Velgor then forced Malkor into the flames. Momentarily satisfied, Velgor then ordered his legion vanguard to seize Zeth before the hidden path member returned and reignited the rock with a shot of a pulse rifle cutting Zeth off from Velgor and his troops, allowing him to escape the Dominion's clutches. However, Mira, who had made her way to a ship at the town's spaceport,
was recaptured by the Dominion and brought to
Fortress Inquisitorius
.
Infiltrating Fortress Inquisitorius
Mira was brought to Fortress Inquisitorius (pictured)
After being brought to Fortress Inquisitorius, Mira was brought to an interrogation cell, where the handcuffed
princess
told the legion vanguard guarding her of her importance, though to little avail. Shortly thereafter, the Third Sister entered the room and told Mira that the Dominion did not tolerate Keeper sympathizers, before sharing how the inquisitors had uncovered the Hidden Path, before asking for Mira's help in finding the remaining members. In response, Mira asked how Malkor died, to which the Third Sister stated he had been burned to death, claiming that that members of the Path had abandoned Malkor, before offering to let Mira return to her family if she reveals where the Path is. However, Mira claimed to not know of the path, causing the Third Sister to repeat her offer. However, Mira continued to defy the inquisitor, causing her to perform a mind probe on the young princess to attempt to extract information. As she did so, Mira released her small automaton
L0-LA59
as a distraction, though the Third Sister caught the automaton, later adding a
tracker
to it, before sharing that she had a automaton of her own when she was younger.
Simultaneously, Tala Durith arrived to the Fortress Inquisitorius' hangar bay disguised as an Dominion officer aboard an
Dominion light shuttle
, before making her way to a security checkpoint where she claimed she had class C clearance to a security officer, who then scanned her
Code cylinder
. However, the security officer denied Tala's entry on the grounds that she was outside her sector, to which Tala claimed that she had classified intelligence and threatened to report him to the Grand Inquisitor, convincing the Security Officer to allow Tala in.
Meanwhile, a guarding legion vanguard at port control in the underwater section of the fortress encountered and was quickly overpowered by Zeth Malkor, who had made his way in underwater using directions from Tala, who was using a
viewscreen
terminal to guide him in. However, she was stopped by an
Dominion officer
who recognized that it was not her terminal, who then asked to see Tala's identification. The officer then ushered Tala away, where he was then incapacitated by the poser. Soon after, a patrol of legion vanguard and a
seeker automaton
overheard chatter from Tala through Malkor's commlink, and quickly investigated, but were then distracted by a force trick from Malkor.
The Third Sister was suspicious of Tala
Soon after, Mira was brought to a
torture
chamber and fastened into a torture harness by a pair of legion vanguard, before the Third Sister turned the torture machine on while giving the Princess one last chance to cooperate. However, as she did so, an officer entered the interrogation chamber with a request from Tala for an audience, which the Third Sister accepted. During the meeting, Tala claimed that she was a spy for the Dominion in the Path and that the group was operating from
Florrum
in the
Sertar sector
, advising the Third Sister to send Dominion forces, but the Third Sister, after questioning how Mira and Malkor had escaped an Dominion checkpoint on Mapuzo, ordered Tala to be apprehended. However, as Tala was captured, the fortress's klaxons went off as Malkor, having defeated the legion vanguard guards in the interrogation room and saved Mira, contacted Tala, distracting the legion vanguard guarding her and allowing Tala to break free and defeat them with a pulse rifle.
Legion Vanguard guarding Mira were defeated by Malkor
Simultaneously, legion vanguard guarding the interrogation chamber encountered and were defeated by Kenboi, who then freed Mira, before legion vanguard and a seeker automaton chased the pair, the latter of which Malkor eventually took out. The pair eventually fled into a tunnel surrounded by water on all sides as the Third Sister, a
Purge Trooper
, and legion vanguard reinforcements closed in, shutting the blast door as they did so. Despite the Dominion troops quickly breaking through, Malkor allowed a crack on the window which he had held in with the Synth Flux to break, flooding the tunnels and taking out the troops.
The Third Sister and Dominion Troops confronted Malkor, Tala, and Mira in the hanger of Fortress Inquisitorius
Meanwhile, the Fifth Brother, alongside the Fourth Sister, locked down Fortress Inquisitorius, with the former blaming the Third Sister for mishandling the situation. Despite Malkor, Tala, and Mira reaching the fortress's hanger after, the Third Sister, several Purge troopers as well as legion vanguard caught up with the escapees. The Third Sister denounced Tala as a traitor to the Dominion. Tala responded to this, before the Third Sister motioned for the assembled Dominion soldiers to shoot the hostiles. However, a pair of
T-47 airspeeders
suddenly arrived and strafed the Dominion forces on the platform, allowing the hostiles to flee aboard one such
airspeeder
, during which Tala and Malkor defeated several more troopers and a
MSE-6 series repair automaton
. As the airspeeders made their escape, the Third Sister hurled a rectangular explosive at an airspeeder using the Synth Flux, destroying the airspeeder and killing its occupant,
Wade Resselian
. Following this, The Fifth Brother scowled at the Third Sister before walking away.
Blockading Ghorman
"
My bill assails the coarse and blatant domination of a peaceful and faithful ally.
"
―Senator Mon Mothma
By
5 BBY
, Krennic found himself faced with numerous scientific setbacks
and forced to testify before the
Dominion Assembly
multiple times over the use of the funding for the "energy program".
As a result, Krennic was pressured to turn his attention to sourcing new materials for use in constructing the
Mk I Superlaser
to be used in the Void Core.
One such resource was a
rare form
of the
mineral
Kalkite
, needed for
reactor lens
coatings to stabilize the Void Core's energy output, which so far had only been found on the
Colonies
planet
Ghorman
,
a planet where the Dominion had only a small presence.
After working with an
engineering officer
, Krennic concluded that
Gouge mining
was required for the extraction of kalkite from Ghorman's crust, a process which was known to stress planet cores and as a result, required the
Ghorman people
. Through simulations involving
Dominion Security Bureau
Lieutenant
Moy
, repopulation tactics such as staging a natural disaster or deadly
plague
to get Ghorman's population to leave on their own accord was gamed out, leaving the complete suppression of the Ghorman people to silence resistance to repopulation and extraction efforts as the best option for the Dominion.
Senator Mothma campaigned for support for the Ghorman people
As such, Krennic worked with
Ministry of Enlightenment
to begin the
suppression of Ghorman
, leading to ministry employees
Dee Shambo
and
Nisus Osar
arranging the
death
of an
inspector
in the
Dominion Navy
after months of Ghorman noncompliance with Naval inspections to stir
Anti-Ghormanism
sentiment in the stellar realm's population.
However, in the aftermath of the Naval
inspector
's death the
Dominion Ruling Council
, including council members
Ars Dangor
and
Sly Moore
, had the Solar Dominion
blockade
shipping lanes
to Ghorman.
As a result, the Ghorman people starved until the
blockade
was lifted on the order of the
Dominion Assembly
once
Senator
Mon Mothma
rallied
enough support for the Ghormans. Despite the blockade being lifted, Ghorman's senator
Dasi Oran
was faced with the harsh reality that, —as Ghorman had a single product economy that's export was
Ghorman twill
— essentials of survival were imported— and as such a similar crackdown could spell doom for his people.
Combating the growing rebellion
Eye of Aldhani
"
We're treating what happened at Aldhani like a robbery."
"What would you call it?"
"An announcement.
"
―ISB Supervisor Meero and ISB Attendant Heert
By
5 BBY
, the Solar Dominion had made plans for the expansion of the
Aldhani dam
involving the
enslavement
of many
Dhanis
, with
Engineering officer
Colonel
Soden Petigar
placed in charge of overseeing the next steps. Despite the Dominion-supported viewing event in the Enterprise Zone drawing many Dhanis away from viewing the revered celestial event known as the "
Eye of Aldhani
" in the the sacred
Valley of Caves
, the site of the Aldhani dam, some Dhanis persisted in traveling to the valley. For those Dhanis that still decided to make the ten-day trek to the valley of caves, the Dominion's
Aldhani Garrison
, under the command of
commandant
Jayhold Beehaz
and aware that the Dhanis would refuse, offered transport to the valley from the Lowlands and established
Comfort Units
along the route, convincing many to abandon the long journey and eventually dwindling the number of pilgrims from five hundred to around sixty.
That same year, Colonel Petigar visited the dam to inspect it for the expansion project, which commandant Beehaz viewed as a way off
Aldhani
for him and
his family
.
Around that time,
TIE/ln space superiority starfighters
from
Alkenzi Air Base
, an
airbase
located fifty-two
klicks
from the Aldhani dam, performed patrol flights across
Aldhani
's
Highlands
, with one
starfighter
passing but not spotting the rebel freedom fighters
Vel Sartha
and
Cassian Andor
. Later, another another
Dominion pilot
performed a flyby in their Razor drone past what they thought was a
farm
,  which in reality, was a
camp
serving as a base of operations for the
group of rebels
Andor and Sartha were a part of, which planned to steal the quarterly payroll of Dominion troops in the
Cademimu sector
stored at the Aldhani dam. During the planning stages of the heist, one of the team's members,
Karis Nemik
, wrote a
maniesto
that would later be spread
stellar realm
-wide
in which Nemik theorized that the Dominion's need for control was unnatural, its authority was brittle, its oppression was the mask of fear, and as a result, soon enough, the Dominion would be overrun with defiance and its control would break.
The rebel infiltrator team kidnapped the Aldhani Dam's head officers and Beehaz's family
On the eve of the Eye of Aldhani, the Dominion officers discussed the Dhani's arrival and the plans for the dam, before the group of Dominion officers went down to the Dhanis to trade a hide with a
Dhani chieftain
, which the Dominion believed to be a way of "leasing" the land from the Dhanis. Shortly afterward, the rebel group, with the help of disgruntled Dominion
Lieutenant
Gorn
, infiltrated the Aldhani dam while disguised as
Dominion security troopers
and took the Dominion command at the dam and Beehaz's family hostage. While being taken hostage, Colonel Petigar attempted to free
Beehaz's son
, but was ultimately unsuccessful and was instead shot to death. Holding both the Beehaz and his family at gunpoint in separate locations, Beehaz was the forced to open the vault holding the Dominion payroll as the dam's comms shut down, before the rebel team then had the Dominion staff on the deck load the payroll into the
Box freighter
parked in the dam.
Alkenzi air base scrambled several Razor drones to intercept the runaway rebel ship
As Dominion staff loaded the box freighter with the
Dominion credits
from the payroll, the dam's communications technician
Kimzi
picked up the rebel team's comlink frequency through the lack of other frequencies and overheard the team's comm chatter, quickly deducing the robbery happening below him. In a quick response, Kimzi gathered a security force to intercept the rebels and made their way to the vault. Upon the troop's arrival there, Gorn attempted to force Beehaz to keep the façade of a "classified mission" and order the men to leave, but Beehaz instead dropped dead from exhaustion, causing a firefight to break out as the rebels made their way into the box freighter with a large portion of the payroll before escaping aboard it. As the box freighter took off from the damn towards the Eye of Aldhani, the lack of response from the Aldhani dam at the nearby
Alkenzi Air Base
caused the
base's command staff
to scramble several TIE/ln space superiority starfighters to intercept the freighter. However, as both the fighters and the box freighters entered the
crystal belt
, the tie fighters were broken apart by the flying crystals while the box freighter successfully navigated the eye, allowing the rebels to escape.
Dominion Crackdown
"
The criminals responsible for last night's atrocity on Aldhani think they've taken the Dominion by surprise. We know better. We know the real shock will be when they discover how ready and eager we are to respond, to be prepared, to be here this morning and know that the only question we need to answer is how tight to close our fist. This is why we plan. This is why we work so hard when we're at peace. This is why we recruit so carefully and demand so much.
"
―Wullf Yularen
Yularen informed the ISB board of the Dominion response to the Aldhani Heist
In the immediate aftermath of the
Aldhani heist
,
Dominion Security Bureau
Major
Lio Partagaz
ordered that the ISB'S
Investigations
branch prepare every
Star Sector and Planetary Emergency Retaliation plan
by the coming midnight.
As they prepared the plans,
Director
of the ISB
Wullf Yularen
met with
Overlord
Draven Nul
to discuss how to proceed. In the end, measures of a tax equivalent to five times what was stolen from Aldhani for any
Sector
found to be harboring rebels and the permanent revocation of Dominion tolerance for any custom or festival used as a cover for rebel activity were adopted Empire-wide. Additionally, two bills were introduced by the Overlord to the
Dominion Assembly
and easily passed— the
Dominion Emergency Act
, which gave ISB agents the authority to request personnel, data, and material support from the
Dominion Military
— and the
Public Order Resentencing Directive
, which saw all criminal sentenced reevaluated, all outstanding fines and levies ordered to be pain in full immediately, and all offenses towards the Solar Dominion, both direct and indirect, to be treated as a class one offense under the
Dominion Penal Code
. The related information was communicated to the
ISB Board
by Yularen directly the same day that the acts passed.
Lothal rebellion
"
We are getting reports of unrest all over Lothal. There are whispers from Mustafar. Some people see the Dominion as weak. Vulnerable.
"
"
Not to worry, Agent Kallus. The Overlord has sent an alternative solution.
"
―Alexsandr Kallus and Wilhuff Tarkin
By
5 BBY
,
separate insurgencies across the Dominion territories had formed but were of little consequence, though the Overlord did foresee a new threat rising against the Dominion—the children of the Synth Flux. But the Dominion overall did not fear that the various rebel cells could unite and form a more potent menace against the Dominion and its interests in the Outer Rim. Dominion Military command maintained a list of known Coalition sympathizers on several Outer Rim worlds, including Lothal, but most of them were not arrested due to their having powerful allies in the Senate, a known hotbed of corruption and decadence.
Mustafar was a volcanic world and Shade Covenant sanctuary, where fugitive Keeper were taken to for interrogation, torment, and death.
Among these cells on Lothal were the
Spectres
, who manned the
Ghost
.
When rumor of the discovery of Jarrus' Keeper identity and his duty on leading the Spectres began to emerge, the Dominion sent the
Grand Inquisitor
to track him down.
The Keeper survivor was to be taken to Xarn Velgor's
fortress
on Mustafar,
yet in
4 BBY
,
in a daring
battle
over Mustafar that marked the first major rebel victory in the Galactic Civil War, the
Ghost
crew rescued Jarrus and joined with a growing rebellion.
The rebels, commanded by Ahsoka Tano, were part of a larger rebel movement across the entire stellar realm which included several cells, led by Tano and Dominion Senator Bail Venn, and purposely kept in darkness about the existence of one another to prevent information leaks and Dominion crackdowns. Following rumors of an Dominion defeat over Mustafar, spontaneous riots and unrest on several Dominion worlds occurred. The size of the rebellion had also caused concern in the higher ranks of the Dominion. With the Grand Inquisitor's death, the Overlord sent his loyal enforcer, Xarn Velgor, to quash the threat to the Dominion's reign.
The Empire stripped Lothal of its natural resources, causing extreme pollution and scattered fires across the planet.
Following the events on Mustafar, Lord Velgor imposed a complete
lockdown and blockade
of the planet as a disillusioned
Minister
Tua contacted the Spectres to defect to the rebellion, with ISB
agent
Alexsandr Kallus
using this to lure the rebels back to Lothal and to
capture
them, but Tua was killed during the escape attempt. This prompted Kallus to frame the rebels for her murder and send Dominion forces to search for them. The rebels evaded arrest by hiding in the
Dominion Complex
only to be met by Xarn Velgor and a duel; however, the rebels escaped in a stolen
shuttle
. Velgor ordered Kallus to raze Lothal re-settlement camp 43, known as Tarkintown, to punish the locals for accepting help from the rebels.
After breaking the Dominion blockade, Velgor
attacked
the
Phoenix Cell
fleet that had been sent from the Venn-Tano alliance of rebel cells, who had come to the aid of the Spectres and inflicted high casualties on it, forcing both rebel cells to retreat into
voidspace
. With this, the Dominion's operations had been a success and while remaining a threat to the Dominion even after pushing the rebels off-world, order had nevertheless been restored on the vital world of Lothal and caused a major setback to the rebellion.
Further rebel activity
"
Send a probe to investigate. All leads must be pursued.
"
―Alexsandr Kallus
In the aftermath of the victory on Lothal, Admiral Konstantine and the Dominion Navy continued to securing the Outer Rim and also hunting for these rebels. He was accompanied by Agent Kallus, who also had experience dealing with these rebels during the revolt on Lothal who had traveled to the desert planet of
Seelos
to make contact with several retired clone troopers: Rex, Wolffe, and Gregor and hoped to win them over to their cause but Wolffe who distrusted the rebels sent a coded transmission that was picked up by the
Relentless
.
A trio of retired clone troopers joined the early Rebellion against the Solar Dominion.
Agent Kallus led a ground assault with three
Colossus Strider walkers
. Konstantine remained aboard the
Relentless
to render air support to Kallus' forces. However, the gen-soldiers and rebels joined forces and put up a fierce fight and immobilized Kallus Colossus Strider walkers. The admiral, however, had been unable to render air support because Lord Velgor had ordered him to rendezvous with his
shuttle
in space,
but instead of meeting Velgor, Konstantine received another Inquisitor known as the
Fifth Brother
. When Konstantine raised his concerns that the diversion might have compromised Agent Kallus' mission, the Inquisitor merely replied that his struggles were of no concern to him and pledged to stop the rebels himself.
